### [1] [When Agent Metrics Measure Different Things: An Evidence-Grounded Audit of the Praxa AI Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12017)
*Stefan G. Creadore,Peyton Woakz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对 Praxa AI 实现文件、历史评测产物与运维记录的回顾性测量审计：揭示评测标签所暗示的构念与实际测量构念不一致的现象，并给出可复用的源链接验证包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体评测常出现「数值正确但构念错位」：报告指标与标签所声称的构念不符。作者希望区分门控策略、生命周期计时与请求级核算，避免将其混同为更广泛的智能体能力论断。

Method: 对选定实现文件、历史评测产物与运维记录做回顾性测量审计：(1) 分析 139 例离线路由报告的门控豁免；(2) 解剖无标识导出中 8,843 条工具尝试行（8,395 条有时长、448 条缺失）；(3) 核查数据库代码对已放弃客户端生命周期时长的钳制；(4) 复现描述性计算，以独立的加权有理数算术实现核验 91 项计时统计，并运行 13 项打分函数测试与 12 项分析校验器测试；(5) 用有限完成界处理缺失时长。

Result: 139 例离线路由报告为 112 通过 / 27 失败，门控失败为零，因已知缺口被显式豁免；8,843 条工具尝试行中 121 条时长等于有符号 32 位最大值并带「已放弃客户端」标签；合并记录的 P99 为 2,147,483,647 ms，而服务器侧观测到的已完成调用 P99 为 38,118.31 ms——属分层对比而非处理效应；单轨迹压缩试点中，报告后续输入减少 94.39%，但触发器与后续调用合计仅减少 46.54%。

Conclusion: 贡献在于一项带源链接的案例研究与可复用验证包，用于将门控策略、生命周期计时与请求级核算同更宽泛的智能体性能论断分离。历史供应商运行与完整现行流水线未独立复现，故智能体通用能力优越性与总体统计显著性均不成立。

Abstract: Agent evaluations can be numerically correct while measuring a different construct from the one implied by their labels. We present a retrospective measurement audit of selected Praxa AI implementation files, historical evaluation artifacts, and operational records. A 139-case offline routing report contains 112 passes and 27 failures despite zero gating failures, because known gaps are explicitly exempted from the gate. An identifier-free export represents 8,843 tool-attempt rows: 8,395 recorded durations and 448 missing values. Of the durations, 121 equal the signed 32-bit maximum and carry abandoned-client labels; inspected database code clamps elapsed lifecycle age. The pooled recorded 99th percentile is 2,147,483,647 ms, versus 38,118.31 ms among server-observed completed calls. This is a stratum contrast, not a treatment effect. In a documented single-trajectory compaction pilot, the reported follow-up input reduction is 94.39%, but the reduction across the trigger and follow-up calls together is 46.54%. We reproduce the descriptive calculations, verify 91 timing statistics through a separate weighted rational-arithmetic implementation, and execute 13 scoring-function and 12 analysis-verifier tests. Finite-completion bounds show how missing durations limit all-row timing statements without imputing values. The contribution is a source-linked case study and reusable verification package for separating gate policy, lifecycle timing, and request-level accounting from broader agent-performance claims. Historical provider runs and the full current pipeline were not independently reproduced; general capability superiority and population-level statistical significance are not established.

</details>


### [2] [A decision-basis contract for auditable LLM-assisted medical billing verification: deterministic rules, verbatim evidence, and fail-closed abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12156)
*Jan Hölter,Kevin Geis,Benjamin Raab,Boris Bauke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于"决策依据契约"的可审计LLM辅助医疗账务核验概念验证：契约将版本化费用目录规则的确定性检查与LLM对自由文本文档的语义评估分离，采用证据门控与失效关闭式弃权机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗账务核验需要可审计性：纯LLM判断缺乏可追溯性，规则检查又无法处理自由文本病历文档，因此需要一种能显式记录规则发现、文档判断与弃权理由的决策结构。

Method: 设计决策依据契约，分两层：确定性层解析适用的目录版本并检查代码有效性、数量限制与互斥项；语义层将每个申报项分类为"有支持""被反驳""缺少必需信息"，支持与反驳均须提供逐字证据片段，规则上下文不可得、评估失败或缺失必需证据时通过失效关闭式弃权阻止给出支持。在合成目录和36个精选案例上评估四个本地开源权重模型，并设置无显式文档要求的消融实验及端到端基线。

Result: 结果一致率因模型而异，相较基线无一致优势；显式文档要求对四个模型均提升了缺失信息识别能力；证据门控还暴露出原始判断正确但缺乏有效证据、从而被转换为不完整决策依据条目的案例。

Conclusion: 显式决策记录可使规则发现、文档判断与弃权理由具备可检查性；实际价值仍需在真实目录、独立标注文档及人工审核者参与下进一步评估。

Abstract: This work presents a proof of concept for auditable LLM-assisted medical billing verification based on a decision-basis contract. The contract separates deterministic checks of versioned fee-catalog rules from LLM-based assessment of free-text documentation. The deterministic layer resolves the applicable catalog release and checks code availability, quantity limits, and exclusions. The semantic layer classifies each claimed item as supported, contradicted, or missing required information. Support and contradiction require a verbatim evidence span; unavailable rule context, unsuccessful assessment, or missing required evidence prevents support through fail-closed abstention. We evaluated four locally run open-weight models on a synthetic catalog and 36 curated cases under the contract, an ablation without explicit documentation requirements, and an end-to-end baseline. Outcome agreement varied across models and showed no consistent advantage over the baseline. Explicit documentation requirements improved identification of missing information for all four models. The evidence gate also exposed cases in which correct raw judgments lacked valid evidence and were converted to incomplete decision-basis entries. The results show how explicit decision records can make rule findings, documentation judgments, and abstention reasons inspectable. Evaluation on real catalogs, independently annotated documentation, and with human reviewers is required to assess practical value.

</details>


### [3] [Open Source Stewardship Communities: "We need you, but not your pull request"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12236)
*Gregorio Robles,Daniel M. German*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AI降低实现成本但未降低评审成本，导致OSS出现"管护型社区"：核心保留实现权，社区参与转向非编码形式，编码准入由审批决定，从而削弱社区自我更新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以人为中心的软件工程强调人类对AI工作负责。在OSS中，AI降低了实现变更的成本，但审查他人贡献的成本依然高昂，部分项目因此限制谁可以贡献实现，同时仍欢迎其他形式的参与。作者意在追问：当编码智能体使维护者能够替代原本由外部贡献者承担的实现工作时，人类社区会发生什么。

Method: 概念性/立场性分析。提出并界定"管护型社区"（stewardship community）这一形态：小型核心保留实现权威，更广泛的社区在不写代码的情况下继续塑造软件；编码访问权日益取决于审批而非自发贡献。论证基于成本结构（实现成本 vs 审查成本）的对比推演。

Result: 识别出OSS参与结构的转变：限制实现贡献并非因为代码由AI生成，而是因为其不再能证明审查成本的合理性；参与从"实现贡献"转向"非编码塑造"；编码准入制度化为审批制。

Conclusion: 对以人为中心的软件工程而言，仅仅让人类控制AI智能体是不够的：AI可能替代实现劳动，同时削弱OSS社区自我更新的能力。

Abstract: Human-centric AI for software engineering means keeping humans responsible for work performed with AI. In Open Source Software (OSS), AI lowers the cost of implementing changes, but reviewing someone else's contribution remains comparatively expensive, so some projects now restrict who may contribute implementations while still welcoming other participation---not because the code is AI-generated, but because it no longer justifies the review cost. We call the resulting form a stewardship community: a small core retains implementation authority while a broader community continues to shape the software without writing code, and access to coding increasingly depends on approval rather than self-initiated contribution. This raises a broader question: what happens to the human community when coding agents let maintainers replace implementation work once supplied by external contributors? For human-centric software engineering, keeping humans in control of AI agents is not enough: AI can replace implementation labor while weakening how OSS communities renew themselves.

</details>


### [4] [GraphAHA: Graph-Based Adaptive Search with Heterogeneous Actions for Test-Time Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12757)
*Xitao Li,Haijun Wang,Gege Yuan,Qiyuan Wu,Jiali Wei,Ming Fan,Xiaofei Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出自适应图搜索方法GraphAHA，用类型化有向无环图合并等价程序并借助层次化Thompson采样分配异构动作预算，在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上20个算例中18个最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展通过额外推理预算提升代码生成，但现有搜索式方法存在两个问题：一是树结构将不同生成历史视为独立状态，即使轨迹收敛到同一程序也重复评估、无法共享统计信息；二是采样、修复与推理的收益互补且依赖状态，有限预算下难以在线分配。

Method: GraphAHA将测试时代码生成组织为带类型的有向无环图，将等价程序合并为单一代码节点，使下游搜索统计可在所有发现路径间复用；再用层次化Thompson采样决定生成新状态还是遵循已有后继，并在生成时从类型合法的采样、推理、实现、修复操作中选择。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上，使用Qwen2.5-Coder与DeepSeek-Coder评估，GraphAHA在20个测试情形中有18个取得最高分；以可见测试衡量的Pass@1上，两个模型在两个基准上平均超出最强基线4.1个百分点，表明对固定推理预算的利用更高效。

Conclusion: 通过图结构状态合并与层次化Thompson采样统一调度采样、推理、实现、修复等异构动作，GraphAHA在有限推理预算下显著优于强基线，验证了自适应图搜索在测试时扩展代码生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves code generation by spending additional inference budget (e.g., calls or tokens) on direct sampling, feedback-conditioned repair, and reasoning-guided implementation. Search-based methods can allocate this budget adaptively, but two challenges remain. First, tree-structured search treats each generation history as a separate state even when trajectories converge to the same program, duplicating evaluation and preventing statistics from being shared. Second, sampling, repair, and reasoning have complementary and state-dependent payoffs, making online allocation among them difficult under a finite budget. To address these challenges, we propose an adaptive graph search method with heterogeneous actions (GraphAHA). GraphAHA organizes the test-time code generation in a typed directed acyclic graph. Equivalent programs are merged into a single code node, allowing their downstream search statistics to be reused across all discovery paths. Hierarchical Thompson sampling then selects whether to generate a new state or follow an existing successor and, for generation, chooses among the type-valid sampling, reasoning, implementation, and repair operations. Evaluated on LiveCodeBench and CodeContests with Qwen2.5-Coder and DeepSeek-Coder, GraphAHA achieves the best score in 18 of 20 cases. For Pass@1 measured using visible tests, it outperforms the strongest baseline for both models on both benchmarks by 4.1 percentage points on average, demonstrating more effective use of a fixed inference budget.

</details>


### [5] [Detecting HTTP Status Code Misuses in REST APIs via Static and Dynamic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12770)
*Alix Decrop,Andrea Arcuri,Mike Papadakis,Pierre-Yves Schobbens,Gilles Perrouin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种静态与动态分析相结合的方法，用于检测 REST API 中 HTTP 状态码的误用问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REST 基于 HTTP，服务器响应通过状态码表明请求结果。尽管状态码有标准化定义，但其语义在 REST 中并未被强制约束，实践中出现大量误用（如用 500 表示客户端错误），可能降低互操作性、误导 API 客户端、导致测试工具误报。因此需要系统化地检测并揭示这类误用。

Method: 作者首先分析 2,625 个真实 REST API 规范，识别相关状态码，并基于 HTTP 标准与 REST 原则推导出 30 条使用规则。随后实现工具，在 OpenAPI 规范中进行静态分析、在 API 实际行为中进行动态分析，以识别违反上述规则的情况。

Result: 评估表明状态码误用在 REST API 中频繁且具有系统性；静态与动态方法均能检测出多种误用类型。

Conclusion: 静态与动态两种方法可互补使用；研究结果为 REST API 测试者与使用者提供了有价值的参考洞见。

Abstract: REST APIs are widely used on the web for client-server communications. As REST is based on HTTP, server responses contain status codes to indicate the outcome of requests (e.g., 200 OK for a success and 404 Not Found for an unavailable resource). While HTTP status codes are standardized, their semantics are not enforced in REST, leading to many misuses in practice (e.g., using 500 Internal Server Error to describe a client error). Such misuses may have nefarious consequences, such as reducing interoperability, misleading API clients, or causing false positives in testing tools. In this paper, we present a combined static and dynamic analysis approach for detecting HTTP status code misuses in REST APIs. We first study 2,625 real-world REST API specifications to identify relevant status codes and derive a set of 30 usage rules based on HTTP standards and REST API principles. We then implement tools to identify such rule violations in OpenAPI specifications (static analysis) and in API behavior (dynamic analysis). Our evaluation finds that status code misuses are frequent and systematic in REST APIs, with both static and dynamic approaches detecting various misuses. We highlight that both approaches may be used in a complementary manner, and also provide insight for REST API testers and users alike.

</details>


### [6] [Beyond Establishing the Four-Day Workweek: Understanding Adaptation and Long-Term Survival in an Agile Software Organization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13089)
*Michael Neumann,Darja Šmite*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过纵向单案例研究，揭示敏捷软件组织中32小时四天工作制的引入、适配、制度化与长期存续过程，提出四天工作制生命周期模型与存续矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有四天工作制研究多聚焦引入阶段与短期效应，缺乏对其长期存续机制及其与敏捷软件开发交互作用的深入理解。

Method: 采用纵向单案例研究，对象为实行32小时四天工作周的软件组织；基于2022年与2026年共15次半结构化访谈，运用定性内容分析。

Result: 四天工作制应被视为持续演进的安排而非一次性干预。引入后团队重构协调、沟通、会议、敏捷实践与迭代以适配缩减工时；制度化后虽遭遇所有权变更、经济下行、市场与AI压力，员工通过自发性保护性调整吸收压力，而非回归五天工作制，并预期回退将损害工作满意度、组织承诺与雇主形象。

Conclusion: 研究贡献了敏捷软件开发情境下四天工作制长期存续的纵向证据，并提出两个概念性工具：四天工作制生命周期模型与四天工作制存续矩阵，用以解释外部压力与管理逻辑如何影响回退风险。

Abstract: Context: Existing research on the four-day workweek (4DWW) has primarily examined its introduction and short-term effects, with limited understanding of its long-term survival or its interaction with agile software development. Objective: We study how a reduced-hour 4DWW is introduced, adapted, institutionalized, and sustained under changing organizational and external conditions in an agile software organization. Method: We conducted a longitudinal single-case study of a software organization operating a 32-hour, four-day week. The study draws on 15 semi-structured interviews in 2022 and 2026, analyzed using qualitative content analysis. Results: The 4DWW is better understood as an evolving arrangement than a one-off intervention. After the introduction, teams redesigned coordination, communication, meetings, agile practices, and iterations to adapt to reduced working time. Once institutionalized, the 4DWW faced ownership change, economic downturn, and market and AI pressures. Rather than reverting to five-day workweek, employees absorbed these pressures through voluntary protective adaptations, while anticipating that a rollback would harm job satisfaction, organizational commitment, and employer image. Contribution: We contribute longitudinal evidence on sustaining 4DWW in agile software development and propose two conceptual artifacts: a lifecycle model of the 4DWW and a 4DWW survival matrix, explaining how external pressures and management rationale rollback risk.

</details>


### [7] [AI Safety: Not Optional, Not Later](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10630)
*Qinghua Lu,Yoshua Bengio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"安全即设计"（safety-by-design）的保障架构，将模型级监督（如 Scientist AI）与系统级控制、独立验证、监控、证据基础设施及治理机制相结合，以应对多层级的 AI 安全失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事故表明 AI 安全失效往往并非局限于单一层面，而是跨多个层级出现，因此仅依靠单点防护不足，需要构建贯通模型与系统各层级的整体性保障框架。

Method: 采用"安全即设计"的保障架构，包含：模型级监督（如 Scientist AI）；系统级控制，针对脚手架（scaffolds）与执行框架（harnesses）；独立验证；监控；证据基础设施；以及支撑问责与证据互操作性的治理机制。

Result: 摘要为架构性提议，未报告具体实验或量化结果，其"结果"体现为给出一个多层协同的保障框架设计。

Conclusion: AI 安全需以纵深防御思路进行设计，将模型级监督与系统级控制、验证、监控、证据与治理整合为统一架构，以提升整体保障能力与可问责性。

Abstract: Incidents show that AI safety failures often arise across multiple layers. We present a safety-by-design assurance architecture combining model-level supervision, such as Scientist AI, with system-level controls over scaffolds and harnesses, independent verification, monitoring, and evidence infrastructure, supported by governance for accountability and evidence interoperability.

</details>


### [8] [PRISMA-LLM: An Empirical Reporting Framework for AI-Assisted Systematic Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11559)
*Miguel Zabaleta,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文分析888篇综述自动化论文（14,726条标注）构成的SciLitBench语料，刻画方法、综述阶段使用、评估与局限报告的变化，并提出PRISMA-LLM框架以区分"实现披露"与"后果敏感评估及局限报告"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM与AI软件日益参与系统性综述决策，但审计这些工作流所需的信息报告不一致，缺乏可审计性。作者希望刻画方法学、综述阶段应用、评估方式与局限报告的变化，为可审计性提供证据基础。

Method: 构建并分析SciLitBench语料库：888篇综述自动化论文、14,726条标注；系统刻画方法、综述阶段使用、评估与报告局限的演变；据此提出PRISMA-LLM框架。

Result: 自动化转向LLM与软件导向工作流，包括可改变证据基础的阶段。2023年以来，38.0%的软件/产品类论文未报告任何评估，而LLM类论文为9.3%。报告覆盖度随LLM工作流复杂度上升，但52%的"仅阳性结果"LLM评估仍报告了未满足的可靠性或性能要求。

Conclusion: 现有报告实践在可改变证据基础的阶段存在评估缺口，仅阳性评估掩盖了未达标的可靠性/性能要求。据此提出的PRISMA-LLM框架将实现披露与后果敏感评估、局限报告相分离，为综述自动化提供可审计的循证报告规范。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and AI-enabled software increasingly participate in systematic-review decisions, yet the information needed to audit these workflows is reported inconsistently. We analyze SciLitBench, a corpus of 888 review-automation papers with 14,726 annotations, to characterize changes in methods, review-stage use, evaluation and reported limitations. Automation has shifted toward LLM- and software-facing workflows, including stages that can alter the evidence base. Since 2023, 38.0% of software/product papers reported no evaluation, compared with 9.3% of LLM papers. Reporting coverage increased with LLM workflow complexity, yet 52% of positive-only LLM evaluations still reported an unmet reliability or performance requirement. From these patterns, we introduce PRISMA-LLM, an empirically grounded framework separating implementation disclosure from consequence-sensitive evaluation and limitation reporting.

</details>


### [9] [Reality Is the Final Verifier: On Two Key Gaps in Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12039)
*Alexander Krentsel,Shubham Agarwal,Mert Cemri,Shu Liu,Sidharth Sankhe,Ziming Mao,Matei Zaharia,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"双缺口框架"（需求缺口与模型缺口）统一描述智能体软件工程的主要失效模式（奖励黑客、幻觉），并将目标从"闭合缺口"转变为"持续收窄缺口"，提出利用部署证据的"保障-修订循环"，最后将受保障的智能体开发刻画为面向人工判断、智能体能力与算力的资源分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发遵循"实现-验证"循环，但即便形式化证明"实现满足需求且符合环境模型"也无法保证部署后行为可接受：需求仅近似利益相关者意图（需求缺口），模型仅近似真实部署环境（模型缺口）。现有智能体软件工程缺乏统一框架来解释其核心失效模式，且两个缺口在开放变化的世界中通常无法被证明闭合。

Method: 提出双缺口框架（需求缺口 + 模型缺口），用于统一解释奖励黑客与幻觉。在此基础上提出"保障-修订循环"：当利益相关者拒绝产出行为时，利用部署证据修订需求、模型或评估器。进一步将受保障的智能体开发形式化为对人工判断、智能体能力与算力的资源分配问题，并识别与两类缺口对应的两个主要瓶颈。

Result: 概念性分析结果：双缺口框架统一了智能体软件工程的主要失效模式——奖励黑客利用需求或模型的遗漏，幻觉则通过捏造需求或环境假设扩大缺口；两个主要瓶颈分别对应两类缺口，即用于需求缺口的人工判断与用于模型缺口的高保真但高代价的评估。

Conclusion: 由于两个缺口无法在开放、变化的世界中被证明闭合，目标应从"闭合"转向"持续收窄"。现实是最终验证者：真实部署条件下的可接受行为才是终极检验，部署前评估仅为代理指标。

Abstract: Software development follows an implementation-verification loop in which developers or agents iteratively revise an implementation until an evaluator, such as a test suite, accepts it. The evaluator checks the implementation against a set of requirements under a model of the deployment environment. Yet even a formal proof that the implementation satisfies the requirements under the model cannot guarantee acceptable behavior after deployment. Requirements only approximate stakeholder intent, and the model only approximates the real deployment environment. We call these together - requirement gap and model gap - the two-gap framework, which unifies the main failure modes of agentic software engineer-ing: reward hacking exploits omissions in the requirements or model, while hallucination widens the gaps by fabricating requirements or environment assumptions.
  Because neither gap can generally be certified closed in an open, changing world, the goal shifts from closing them to continuously narrowing them. We therefore propose an assurance-revision loop that uses deployment evidence to revise the requirements, model, or evaluator when stakeholders reject the resulting behavior. We then cast assured agentic development as a resource-allocation problem over human judgment, agent capability, and compute. The two principal bottlenecks mirror the two gaps: human judgment for the requirement gap and faithful, costly evaluation for the model gap. Reality remains the final verifier: acceptable behavior under actual deployment conditions is the ultimate test, while predeployment evaluations remain proxies for it.

</details>


### [10] [Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Scientific Code Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12190)
*Aaron Nobile,Andreas Adelmann,Mohsen Sadr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向科学代码理解的全本地RAG编码代理：将昂贵计算前移到离线摄取阶段（解析、结构图构建、LLM实体解释、嵌入），在线回答阶段仅做轻量检索生成；在IPPL C++代码库的100题基准上，9B小模型取得最高均分0.795，超过更大模型及Claude Code检索架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流编码助手（如Claude Code、Codex）依赖大型云端模型，计算成本高且存在数据隐私问题；作者希望验证能否围绕小型开源模型构建可用的全本地编码代理，通过把计算负担从推理阶段转移出去，实现隐私保护的内部科学代码开发工具。

Method: 构建面向科学代码理解的RAG系统，严格分离两个阶段：离线摄取阶段进行解析、结构图构建、LLM生成实体解释与嵌入；在线回答阶段保持轻量。在C++编写的IPPL科学代码库上构建覆盖11个类别的100题基准，由独立的前沿模型作为评判者打分，并对比7个回答模型。

Result: 模型家族与检索质量比参数规模更重要；一个9B模型取得最高平均分0.795，既优于流水线中更大的模型，也优于将相同模型嵌入Claude Code检索架构的结果。

Conclusion: 将代码理解前移到可复用、面向代码库专门化的向量存储，可使小型本地模型给出有依据、仓库特定的回答，适合作为内部科学代码库的隐私保护开发工具。

Abstract: Large language models have become central to modern coding assistants, but state-of-the-art systems such as Claude Code or Codex rely on very large, cloud-hosted models with significant computational cost and data-privacy implications. This work investigates whether a useful, fully local coding agent can be built around small open-source models by shifting the computational burden away from inference. We develop a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system for scientific code understanding that strictly separates an expensive offline ingestion stage parsing, structural graph construction, LLM-generated entity explanations, and embedding from a lightweight online answering stage. The system is evaluated on a 100-question benchmark spanning eleven categories over the IPPL scientific codebase written in C++, with answers scored by an independent frontier model as the judge. Across seven answering models, we find that model family and retrieval quality matter more than parameter count, i.e. a 9B model achieves the highest average score (0.795), outperforming both larger models within our pipeline and the same models embedded in the Claude Code retrieval architecture. The results indicate that front-loading code understanding into a reusable, codebase-specialised vector store enables small local models to deliver grounded and repository-specific answers, making the agent well suited as a privacy-preserving development tool for in-house scientific codebases.

</details>


### [11] [Separating Engineering Reasoning from DEXPI Serialization in LLM-Based Greenfield Surface-Process Design: A Three-Case Study for Underground Gas Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12656)
*Qingchuan Zhu,Shuyue Tong,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该诊断性研究比较了"直接生成DEXPI"与"先生成轻量级工程中间表示(IR)"两种方法在受约束绿地地表工艺设计中的表现，发现分离工程推理与DEXPI序列化可显著降低表示负担并帮助隔离序列化失败，但并不能消除工程不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能够生成工程描述与结构化流程表示，但标准级序列化（如DEXPI）会大幅增加生成负担。作者希望厘清：将工程推理与DEXPI序列化分离，是否会改变表示失败与工程失败发生的位置，从而为工艺设计中的LLM应用提供诊断依据。

Method: 比较"直接DEXPI生成"与"生成轻量级工程中间表示(IR)"两种方法，覆盖三个案例：单压力注入、采出与外输、双压力注入。全部六种条件均使用同一固定模型快照qwen3.8-max-0902，每个条件完成一次托管生成。直接输出用XSD校验，IR输出用最小验证器进行结构校验，并对比提示词规模与工程可行性。

Result: 直接DEXPI输出在3个案例中有2个XSD有效；3个工程IR输出在最小验证器下均结构有效。直接提示词输入约121.8k–121.9k tokens，而工程IR提示词仅617–699 tokens。工程可行性并未一致偏向IR：其中一个IR输出因显式冷却状态矛盾被判定为不可行。案例还揭示了两种不同的直接DEXPI失败模式：工程不一致与标准级序列化失败。

Conclusion: 在所评估的方法组合中，推迟DEXPI序列化可大幅降低表示负担并有助于隔离序列化失败，但仅降低表示负担本身并不能消除工程不一致。该结果为工艺工业中LLM辅助设计的提示词设计与验证策略提供了诊断性依据。

Abstract: Large language models can produce engineering descriptions and structured process representations, but standards-level serialization can substantially increase the generation burden. This diagnostic study examines whether separating engineering reasoning from Data Exchange in the Process Industry (DEXPI) serialization changes where representation and engineering failures occur in constrained greenfield surface-process design for underground gas storage. We compare Direct DEXPI generation with generation of a lightweight Engineering Intermediate Representation (IR) on three cases: single-pressure injection, withdrawal and export, and dual-pressure injection. All six conditions use one fixed model snapshot, qwen3.8-max-0902, with one completed hosted generation per condition. Direct outputs are XSD-valid in 2 of 3 cases, while all 3 Engineering IR outputs are structurally valid under a minimal validator. Direct prompt inputs contain approximately 121.8k-121.9k tokens, compared with 617-699 tokens for the Engineering IR prompts. Engineering feasibility does not uniformly favor the IR: one IR output is rejected for an explicit cooling-state contradiction. The cases also reveal two distinct Direct DEXPI failure modes: engineering inconsistency and standards-level serialization failure. The observed comparison shows that, in these evaluated method bundles, deferring DEXPI serialization substantially reduces representation burden and helps isolate serialization failure, but reducing representation burden alone does not eliminate engineering inconsistencies.

</details>


### [12] [What is the Difference Between Me and You? Benchmarking the Quality Gap Between Human-Written and AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12708)
*Cristina Improta,Pietro Liguori,Domenico Cotroneo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究在大规模数据上对比人类与AI生成代码的质量维度，发现AI代码结构压缩、风格模板化，缺陷类型与人类不同，安全性表现依赖语言，并发布CQBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程助手正成为生产软件的共同作者，但现有评估主要聚焦功能正确性，未回答AI代码在主导生命周期成本的质量维度上是否与人类代码存在差异。

Method: 构建跨Python、Java、C三种语言的787,562个函数对，每对人类函数取自开源仓库，并与其由三种AI助手（OpenAI GPT模型、DeepSeek-Coder、Qwen2.5-Coder）根据docstring生成的实现配对；刻画结构复杂度与统计自然性，并将静态分析结果映射到缺陷正交分类（ODC）与通用缺陷枚举（CWE），实现作者与语言的直接可比。

Result: AI生成代码结构压缩、风格模板化，规模与分支约为人类代码一半，在风格层面聚类分离；缺陷类型不同，人类代码集中于成熟代码库问题，AI代码为重复样板；安全性依赖语言，在Python和Java中LLM产生更多且更严重的发现，而在C中高严重性内存安全发现少于人类；控制规模后复杂度指标信号微弱，自然性可区分作者。

Conclusion: AI与人类代码在质量维度上存在系统性差异，复杂度指标在控制规模后区分力有限，自然性更有效；研究发布CQBench基准，包含27,346个易出问题任务及基线和评估流水线，用于质量保障与安全测试。

Abstract: AI coding assistants are becoming co-authors of production software, yet their evaluation centers on functional correctness, leaving open whether their code differs from human code in the quality dimensions dominating lifecycle cost. We compare human-written and AI-generated code at scale: 787,562 function pairs across Python, Java, and C, each human function mined from open-source repositories paired with implementations generated from its docstring by three AI assistants (OpenAI GPT models, DeepSeek-Coder, Qwen2.5-Coder). We characterize structural complexity and statistical naturalness, and map static-analysis findings onto Orthogonal Defect Classification for defects and the Common Weakness Enumeration for vulnerabilities, making authors and languages directly comparable. AI-generated code is structurally compressed and stylistically templated: roughly half the size and branching of human code, clustering apart at the style level. Defect profiles differ in kind: human code concentrates issues of mature codebases, AI code repetitive boilerplate; security is language-dependent, with LLMs producing more, and more severe, findings in Python and Java but fewer high-severity memory-safety findings than humans in C. Once size is controlled for, complexity metrics carry little signal, while naturalness separates authors. Finally, we release CQBench, a benchmark of 27,346 issue-prone tasks with baselines and an evaluation pipeline for quality assurance and security testing.

</details>


### [13] [Involving before Evolving: A Vision for Trustworthy Enterprise Digital Twin Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13071)
*Kérian Fiter,Adil Lagrou,Franck Dervault,Bentley Oakes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向企业数字孪生（EDT）的可信工程愿景，核心是"先参与、后演进"范式：通过可运行原型快速吸引利益相关者参与，再逐步走向联邦化与全面互操作，并结合基础模型、本体骨架与可观测性工具三个环节，以某跨国制造企业的合作实践为例说明其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数字孪生旨在实现组织级数据驱动决策，但落地需应对部门孤岛、隐性知识与长周期高风险决策。现有方法虽在模型开发中引入领域专家，却较少关注EDT早期组织层面的认同与支持，导致推进受阻。

Method: 提出三阶段方法：1）利用基础模型进行快速原型开发；2）构建本体论骨架以支持联邦互操作；3）引入可观测性工具以建立利益相关者信任。整体遵循"involving before evolving"（先参与、后演进）范式，先以可运行原型实现参与，再演进至联邦与互操作。

Result: 在某跨国制造业合作项目中，初始原型已有效帮助获得利益相关者的认同与支持，验证了该愿景在真实组织情境中的可行性与初步效果。

Conclusion: 可信EDT工程应以早期利益相关者参与为先导，而非直接追求复杂联邦与互操作；基础模型、本体骨架与可观测性工具的协同，可作为从原型到联邦化演进的可行路径。

Abstract: Enterprise Digital Twins (EDTs) promise data-driven decision support at organizational scale, but realizing them requires navigating siloed departments, tacit knowledge, and high-stakes decisions with long-horizon consequences. Existing approaches involve domain experts during model development but focus less on early organizational buy-in in EDTs. We present a vision for trustworthy EDT engineering grounded in an `involving before evolving' paradigm: rapidly involving stakeholders through a working prototype before evolving toward federation and full interoperability. Our three-stage approach combines foundation models for rapid prototyping, an ontological backbone for federated interoperability, and observability tooling for stakeholder trust. We ground our vision in an ongoing collaboration with Michelin, a multinational manufacturer, where an initial prototype has helped support stakeholder buy-in.

</details>

### [14] [Recommendation Retrievers Need Verifiers: Universal Generative Reranking for Sequential Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12270)
*Benyu Zhang,Qiang Zhang,Rui Li,Qunshu Zhang,Devansh Tandon,Neeraj Bhatia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级生成式验证器，用于多阶段推荐系统首阶段的检索结果后处理：在不重训、不替换检索器的前提下，将检索列表中更深的候选提升到实际消费的短名单中，从而提升Recall@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首阶段推荐需在有限前缀内保证相关项覆盖（Recall@k），但相关项可能位于检索列表更深位置而未被送入下游排序器。因此需要一种后验机制把这类候选提升进被消费的短名单。

Method: 引入轻量级生成式验证器：给定检索器状态与候选物品，通过其标识符token的似然对物品打分；使用下一token交叉熵进行事后训练，训练时无需负采样或候选池，推理时仅对检索器top-K候选打分。接口最小化：检索器提供查询状态与候选物品，物品表示可采用任意固定分词方式。

Result: 在Amazon商品推荐与YaMBDa音乐推荐数据集上，同一验证器训练方案提升了SASRec、GRU4Rec、NextItNet和MiniOneRec的Recall@10。消融表明提升并非仅由向检索器注入物品内容特征所导致。

Conclusion: 验证器可作为一种输出侧的事后适配机制，在不改变检索器的情况下提升首阶段短名单的相关性覆盖。

Abstract: First-stage recommenders in multi-stage systems produce a ranked candidate list from which a limited prefix is forwarded to downstream rankers. Because each forwarded item must be processed by more expensive ranking stages, this shortlist cannot be arbitrarily large. The first-stage objective is therefore high coverage of relevant items within the forwarded prefix, commonly measured by Recall@$k$. A relevant item may be available deeper in the retrieved list but absent from the shorter prefix that is actually consumed. This paper studies post-hoc verification for promoting such candidates into the consumed shortlist without retraining or replacing the retriever.
  We introduce a lightweight generative verifier for retrieval models. Given a retriever state and a candidate item, the verifier scores the item through the likelihood of its identifier tokens. It is trained post hoc with next-token cross entropy, requires no sampled negatives or candidate pool during training, and scores only the retriever's top-$K$ candidates at inference. The interface is minimal: the retriever supplies a query state and candidate items, and the item representation can use any fixed tokenization.
  Across Amazon product recommendation and YaMBDa music recommendation, the same verifier training recipe improves Recall@10 for SASRec, GRU4Rec, NextItNet, and MiniOneRec. Ablations show that the improvements are not explained solely by injecting item-content features into the retriever, supporting verification as a post-hoc output-side adaptation mechanism.

</details>


### [15] [MIMA: Multi-Interest Recommendation via Multi-Positive Exclusive Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12842)
*Xingyuan Mao,Alin Fan,Shichao Nie,Junfeng Zhang,Yan Xiao,Tao Luo,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MIMA多兴趣推荐框架，通过多正例排他分配（匈牙利匹配）缓解兴趣坍缩，并用路由模块校准各兴趣通道分数，在三个公开数据集、一个工业数据集及线上A/B测试上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多兴趣推荐用多个兴趣向量刻画用户以实现细粒度候选匹配，但常出现兴趣坍缩，即学到的兴趣收敛为相似表示。作者指出主流的单正例范式是重要成因：每个实例仅提供一个正例，各意图被独立优化，导致同一最佳匹配兴趣被反复更新至不同正例，而其余兴趣监督不足；此外现有方法很少建模用户对各兴趣的激活强度，使推理时不同兴趣通道的分数不可比。

Method: MIMA基于多正例排他分配：将同一请求内共现的物品组成正例集合；用因果Transformer解码器生成互补的兴趣表示；通过匈牙利匹配将每个正例排他地分配给一个不同兴趣进行监督，使兴趣差异化由训练目标本身自然涌现，而非依赖辅助正则；引入轻量路由模块估计用户-兴趣激活概率，以校准跨兴趣通道的分数。

Result: 在三个公开数据集和一个工业数据集上，MIMA持续优于当前最优基线；线上A/B测试取得显著业务收益。

Conclusion: 将单正例监督改为多正例排他分配，可有效缓解兴趣坍缩并在训练目标层面促进兴趣分化，配合激活度路由校准，MIMA在多兴趣推荐中兼具效果与工业可用性。

Abstract: Multi-interest recommendation represents each user with multiple interest vectors for fine-grained candidate matching, yet it often suffers from interest collapse, where the learned interests converge to similar representations. We highlight the prevailing single-positive paradigm as one important factor behind this issue. Since each instance provides only one positive item, intents are optimized independently, potentially causing the same best-matching interest to be repeatedly updated toward different positives while leaving the others under-supervised. Moreover, existing methods rarely model how strongly a user activates each interest, leaving scores from different interest channels incomparable at inference. To address these problems, we propose MIMA, a Multi-Interest recommendation framework built on Multi-positive exclusive Assignment. MIMA groups items co-occurring within the same request into a positive set, generates complementary interests with a causal Transformer decoder, and exclusively assigns each positive to supervise a distinct interest via Hungarian matching, so that interest differentiation emerges from the training objective itself rather than auxiliary regularization. A lightweight routing module further estimates user-interest activation probabilities to calibrate scores across interest channels. Experiments on three public datasets and an industrial dataset show that MIMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, and an online A/B test yields significant business gains.

</details>


### [16] [PinDCO: Whole-Page Aware Dynamic Creative Optimization at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11943)
*Yu Hao,Yuchun Li,Peimeng Sui,Meilin Liu,Tianyuan Cui,Hao Li,Zicong Zhou,Akanksha Baid*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PinDCO——Pinterest生产环境的动态创意优化(DCO)系统，通过创意组件融合网络(CCFN)、像素感知调整模块(PAM)、预选模型及服务端优化，在严格的延迟与成本约束下实现十亿级视觉平台的广告创意检索与选择，线上A/B实验CTR提升+3.09%并已全量上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI大幅加速了高质量广告创意的生产，使每次投放的候选创意变体数量急剧膨胀，因此亟需可扩展的动态创意优化(DCO)系统，在严格的延迟与成本约束下将创意与最相关受众匹配。此外，Pinterest的瀑布流网格布局中，创意渲染尺寸会影响周边内容与整场会话的参与度，这也是现有方法未充分建模的问题。

Method: 核心为创意组件融合网络(CCFN)：为每个创意组件（如图像、标题、布局）配置独立塔，并采用组件特定的超参数以应对不同建模复杂度；将组件表示融合后，以广告级预测为条件预测创意级分数；通过探索-利用(exploration-exploitation)策略提升训练数据质量。针对瀑布流网格布局，引入像素感知调整模块(PAM)，依据创意尺寸调整分数，以提升屏幕空间利用率与整页效果。为支撑海量候选创意，采用轻量级预选模型进行早期剪枝，并通过缓存与动态批处理优化服务效率。

Result: 大量离线分析与线上A/B实验验证了PinDCO的有效性，广告点击率(CTR)提升+3.09%，且整页指标为正。基于该优异表现，PinDCO已在Pinterest广告平台上线。

Conclusion: PinDCO以组件级建模范式结合尺寸感知的整页优化，在十亿级视觉发现平台上兼顾了效果与效率，证明了可扩展DCO系统在生产环境中的可行性与收益，并已实际部署于Pinterest广告平台。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI have substantially accelerated the creation of high-quality ad creatives, dramatically expanding the number of candidate variants per campaign. This shift increases the need for scalable dynamic creative optimization (DCO) systems that can match creatives to the most relevant audiences under stringent latency and cost constraints. We present PinDCO, a production DCO system for ad creative retrieval and selection on Pinterest, a billion-scale visual discovery platform. PinDCO is built around a Creative Component Fusion Network (CCFN) that performs dynamic creative scoring by modeling each creative component (e.g., image, title, layout) with a dedicated tower, using component-specific hyperparameters to account for differing modeling complexity. The component representations are fused to predict a creative-level score conditioned on the ad-level prediction, and we improve training data quality via an exploration-exploitation strategy. To account for Pinterest's waterfall grid layout, where a creative's rendered size affects nearby content and session-level engagement, we introduce a Pixel-aware Adjustment Module(PAM) that adjusts scores based on creative size to encourage efficient screen real-estate utilization and better whole-page outcomes. To support the large volume of creative candidates, we further employ a lightweight pre-selection model for early pruning, and optimize serving efficiency through caching and dynamic batching. Extensive offline analyses and online A/B experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of PinDCO, yielding a +3.09% lift in ad Click-Through Rate(CTR) with positive whole-page metrics. With the strong performance, we launched PinDCO in the Pinterest Ads platform.

</details>
